============================================================
2. PARSE ALL mode-* VALUES
============================================================
mode-charger: raw=<0x01> value=1
mode-recovery: raw=<0x02> value=2
mode-bootloader: raw=<0x03> value=3
mode-dm-verity-dev-corrupt: raw=<0x04> value=4
mode-kpoc: raw=<0x05> value=5
mode-ddr-reserve: raw=<0x06> value=6
mode-meta: raw=<0x07> value=7
mode-rpmbpk: raw=<0x08> value=8

DT_MODE_NORMAL=ABSENT
DT_MODE_RECOVERY=2
DT_MODE_BOOTLOADER=3
mode-charger: raw=<0x01> value=1
mode-recovery: raw=<0x02> value=2
mode-bootloader: raw=<0x03> value=3
mode-dm-verity-dev-corrupt: raw=<0x04> value=4
mode-kpoc: raw=<0x05> value=5
mode-ddr-reserve: raw=<0x06> value=6
mode-meta: raw=<0x07> value=7
mode-rpmbpk: raw=<0x08> value=8

DT_MODE_NORMAL=ABSENT
DT_MODE_RECOVERY=2
DT_MODE_BOOTLOADER=3

1629:[KERNEL_RESTART_RECOVERY] android17-hidl-fixed-build-20261002/slot-b-first-boot-recovery-cause-audit-20261003/summary.txt:3460
1630-    
1631-    DECISIVE EXISTING EVIDENCE:
1632-    - a retained log showing B/LK mode 0 before a later B/LK mode 2;
1633-  > - a kernel/init line such as "Restarting system with command 'recovery'";
1634-    - sys.powerctl=reboot,recovery;
1635-    - an init service with reboot_on_failure that can execute during early B boot.
1636-    
1637-
1638-------------------------------------------------------------------------

1643-  > - sys.powerctl=reboot,recovery;
1644-    - an init service with reboot_on_failure that can execute during early B boot.
1645-
